# Sprint 53 — The Register

- **author:** [Arthur](../../../../.claude/library/..teamsmanship/..team/arthur/arthur-or-the-shape-of-everything/.cover.md)
- **resource for:** [Sprint 53 — The Annotative Theme](57-sprint-53--the-annotative-theme.md#the-register)

---

*Every member of the 63 files of `.public/package/src` with its three lines under the global criterion — what it depends on · was that designed for it · did the base bend — and its provenance (the first-introducing commit, and whether the run was Doug-driven or long self-guided, read off the sprint records). Produced 2026-09-08 by nine readers (workflow wf_2edc89e5-742), each told to refute. A verdict here is a READING to be checked at the line, never a ruling; `delete-per-ruling` cites the ruling, `cruft` and `design-problem` are the readers' claims.*


**927 members: keep 601 · design-problem 150 · cruft 85 · delete-per-ruling 91.**


## Cluster — src/book (120 members)

**Cross-cutting:** ONE GAP PAID REPEATEDLY. Every class here extends $Composition and restates by hand what its type confers at runtime, because an implements clause cannot be satisfied by a type (designing/21:47-49): heading() is byte-identical in Author.tsx:12 and Subject.tsx:12 (six copies across src per ch21); title() and get meaning in CatalogueCard.tsx:12,18 are byte-identical to reference/IndexCard.tsx:14,20; and the heading-synthesis bond (Author.tsx:16-19, Subject.tsx:16-19, Title.tsx:25-28) is one ruled fact — 'write title to create the heading since it is a section' (designing/20:59) — built three times by hand AND a fourth time in SectionSpecification.supplies (Section.tsx:75-91), which AuthorSpecification/SubjectSpecification inherit through $Annotation.supplies (Annotation.tsx:16). The hand copies exist because $Writing.view() draws the block (Writing.tsx:59) and not the parts; they rebuild _block in the bond, which Sprint 48 D1 chose against (52:47-49). Under T5 (57:44) this is one mechanism on the type, not five sites. THREE $saysSomething WAIVERS, ONE REASON. Synopsis.tsx:24, Index.tsx:37 and Footer.tsx:22 silence WritingSpecification.$saysSomething (Writing.tsx:122) so the EMPTY apparatus $Book.placed() manufactures (Book.tsx:57-65 via $check(kind,'!') and following()'s _block splice, 117-127) is valid. Only Index's has a ruling ('a parenthetical index is made out of what a book sees', 43:216; 'Have document create one… stick it in its block', 35:143). Synopsis's contradicts 'WRITTEN, marked, never generated' (03:20); Footer's is ch21's own example — a chapter manufactured so the book's $endsWithFooter passes (designing/21:13,29). T8 today deletes placed()'s block slices ('We do NOT cache lots of blocks', 57:47), which removes the reason for both. The three are also spelled three ways (with/without the unused `writing` parameter). THE DRAWN CHAIN IS NOT THE BUILT CHAIN. Doug drew '$IndexCard (reference) → $CatalogueCard (book) → $Title, $Subject, $Author' (43:396) and 'Title, Author and Subject are the cards of the card catalogue… Arguably they are all $$' (50:33, U13 design owed 50:34). Built: $TypeOfTitle/$TypeOfAuthor/$TypeOfSubject extend $TypeOfSection (Title.tsx:40, Author.tsx:23, Subject.tsx:23); $CatalogueCard has no descendants, no users in src/.wiki/.tests, and since 012283d confers nothing $IndexCard does not (the demo's $Project extends $IndexCard directly, .wiki/.encyclopedia/.chapter.tsx:78). The name+lines vs title distinction Doug gave (47:245) collapsed in code. $carriesTitle (CatalogueCard.tsx:28) is subsumed by the inherited $titleMeansSomething (IndexCard.tsx:40). COUPLINGS. Index.tsx:7 is the only base-folder import of encyclopedia/ in these eight files (ColumnsFormat) — delete per R3 (57:60). CatalogueCard.tsx:6 → reference/IndexCard.tsx:7 → book/Title is a book↔reference cycle, accepted as W4 (50:214); T9's four doors (57:48) will have to place it. UNUSED AND UNTESTED. Abstract and CatalogueCard have zero consumers outside src/index.ts (grep src, .wiki, .tests, this session); Footer and Index are named by no test; the demo defines its own $Footer extends $Chapter and $FooterFormat (.wiki/.article/.margin.tsx:13,59) rather than use book/Footer — an extension routing around the base, which 57:29 says to file against the base. Abstract.tsx:10 copies parenthetical = true from Synopsis with no ruling that an abstract hides; Author.tsx:29 reads 'a author'. RUN CHARACTER. The v2.2 shape of all eight files is 65b7053 (Sprint 45, 2026-09-05), the port whose brief was 'copy, assess based on the file pattern and adjust' (47:10) — the template members are sound because the template is Doug's (47:239-252), and the copies/waivers/redundant rules are where the port over-applied it. Abstract (012283d) and Footer (ad7ec35) were born in the two 2026-09-07 sessions Doug attended but which the record itself marks as 'the third instance of the same fault tonight' (50:259). The two members with the cleanest provenance — Synopsis.parenthetical (882144a, signed off at review 09:368) and Index.parenthetical (d60e928, 'all his words' 36:119) — are Doug's and keep.

## Cluster — library/.public/package/src/writing (65 members)

**Cross-cutting:** Five kinds, one shape, and the same four costs paid across them. (1) The class/type split: every kind here extends $Composition while its INTERFACE extends $Section$ or $Paragraph$ and its TYPE extends $TypeOfSection/$TypeOfParagraph — so whatever $Section and $Paragraph the CLASSES do (Section.tsx:20 reading() = reflection.wrapped; Section.tsx:24-26 the bond writing mentions onto paragraphs; Paragraph.tsx:22-26 frame wearing ProseFormat) never reaches $Table, $Summary, $Heading, $List or $Illustration; the type says 'a table is a section' and the class says it is not. Ruling 21 row 3 is this gap at its cheapest — heading() copied byte-identical into $Table (Table.tsx:20) and $Summary (Summary.tsx:13) — and two rules pay it worse: $columnsDivideCells (Table.tsx:44) and $showsSomething (Illustration.tsx:48) guard on the CLASS with instanceof, so a writing that merely carries the type is skipped by the first and REFUSED by the second, against 14-shells-over-types 'the type grants the powers'; and $Summary.parenthetical (Summary.tsx:11, also Abstract/Index/Synopsis) is a fact declared on the class that the type does not confer (37-the-binder.md:58 measured it). (2) Base folder coupled to theme folder: four of five files import an encyclopedia Format — Heading.tsx:8 HeadingFormat, List.tsx:8 BulletsFormat, Table.tsx:9 TableFormat, Illustration.tsx:8 IllustrationFormat — all four introduced by the v2.2 port (65b7053) or Sprint 48 (ad7ec35), none by a ruling; Sprint 52 §what-the-folders-mean names writing/ as 'the model, shared by every theme'. Under today's ruling three go (the kind writes <h2>, <ul>, <figure>) and TableFormat stays as the structural exception (grid + display: contents, ruling 20:274) — though TableFormat.tsx:10-16 also carries the encyclopedia's look, which is the sheet's. Only Summary is clean, because it draws nothing. Dead member found on the way: HeadingFormat.$id (HeadingFormat.tsx:6) — $Heading passes 'id' and nothing reads '$id' (grep: one site). (3) Three views read copy by hand with html.text(this._block) — Heading.tsx:22, List.tsx:18, Illustration.tsx:19 — where the base owns that reading as $view() (Writing.tsx:70-73); all three draw $(this._block) rather than $(this.reading()) (Writing.tsx:38,59 — the seam Sprint 51 added) and all three skip $Writing.view()'s meaning→Anchor path (Writing.tsx:57-67). $List's is the harmful one: it re-parses flattened copy with a regex instead of reading its parts, and 50-sprint-46 W10/W11 record the destruction it causes (a <Ref> in an item draws zero; two items merge on a JSX brace). (4) The bond idiom `$check(block, $Block).concat($check($TypeOfX, '!'))` is in 29 src files (grep); its first half repeats Writing.tsx:53, and Sprint 52 §what-markdown-does-not-solve put a number on the idiom ($Eval 6,421 and $Block 3,802 per page load) without a ruling on it. Provenance by run: what Doug ruled live is the kinds themselves and their facts — $List and $Table (35-the-margin.md:108-111, ten rounds), $columns and columns-divide-cells (R110, 40-sprint-38:40), Heading split from Title (43-sprint-41:5,394), Summary parenthetical (03-sprint-46:20), the table's frame and the heading's anchor out of the 2026-09-06 demo rulings (20:235,274). What the v2.2 port (65b7053, long self-guided) added on its own is the four Format imports, both heading() copies, cells()'s body, and $noBlankLine's stand-down (cruft: it silences a blank-line rule that JSX-authored copy cannot trip, 20:255); and Illustration (ad7ec35) landed whole with $source/source/caption/$showsSomething in a Sprint 48 commit whose record never names it, disagreeing with the one ruling on it (R-A17/R-A18, 41-sprint-39:42: Section-kind, subclass-overridable drawn(), a caption rule) on level, mechanism and rule. One contradiction on record for Doug: 35-the-margin.md:111 says a table needs no title and Table.tsx:50 obeys it; 43-sprint-41:396 says that silencing was deleted; the port restored it — best idea wins, and it is his. Enforcement is thin: .tests names List's li count (writing.test.tsx:172) and Table's grid (:175-186) and nothing else in these five files — no promise for Illustration, Summary, $noBlankLine, $showsSomething, $columnsDivideCells or the stood-down heading (grep verified). The slug expression at Heading.tsx:22 is now ONE site — Sprint 46's P8 was met by deleting the Book.tsx copy (grep: no hit).

## Cluster — library/.public/package/src/book (56 members)

**Cross-cutting:** The cluster is three reader's marks (Bookmark = point, Highlight = range, PageFold = page) that Doug ruled as 'one block of work' (35-the-margin.md:32) and the checklist left open as M5 'the three are one family — reader's marks — and no kind names them' (48-the-v2-2-checklist.md:170); 00-planning.md:189 generalises them as 'a reference whose address is its POSITION, not its cargo' and marks the redesign NEEDS DESIGN (Doug, 2026-09-04). ZERO CALLERS: none of the three is referenced by src beyond index.ts:45-47, by any .tests promise, or by .wiki — their v2.1 .spec examples (src/tests/.spec/book/*) died at 65b7053 and nothing replaced them, though 48:168 ticks V1–V7 for all three. ONE GAP PAID THREE WAYS: $Reference is designed for a mark that carries its address, so each kind hand-rolls the ask the framework lacks — chapter() walks ancestors with instanceof $Chapter (no 'nearest ancestor by type' ask; 21-polymorphic-limiting.md:46), pair() filters the block with instanceof $Reference (bypassing searchFor, Writing.tsx:80; P34 19:127; T4 17:75), $sameKind parses a kind:position grammar Doug deleted (W5 50:215, D-K 50:578; c8a29a2 swept four files and missed this one — no 'what level does a path name' ask exists), and location holds a number for a mechanism brainstormed (35 § the fold's position) and never built. PERSIST IS CONFERRED WHERE IT CANNOT ACT: the seat is ruled (P13 19:59, 14:27, 38:23) but $Writing.specify() is called only from .tests and build/stages/validate.ts:63, never by the served demo — v2.1's 'specifically CALLED IN THE CONSTRUCTOR' (35:198, Doug's 'we can't do that if specifically is not actually run' 35:79) was dropped by the v2.2 port — and a bookmark standing in a chapter (the case its own rule waives) gets $pid = '' (Reference.tsx:35, Html.ts:6), which hydration.pidOf reads as none (hydration.ts:53-54); both specifically overrides also narrow the parameter to the kind's class while touching only persist, an assumption the base never verifies (21 rule 3). THE PORT'S SILENT DROPS: 65b7053 (long-self-guided by the register's rule) deleted $Highlight.read() — R59's ruled observable 'a highlight re-follows to exactly its letters' (35:51) — leaving beginning()/ending() orphaned and $Reference.read() throwing on any pair; 012283d rebased both $TypeOfBookmark and $TypeOfPageFold off $TypeOf$Chapter without a recorded ruling, against Doug's 'Maybe the bookmark is a type of $chapter?' (35:91) and leaving S12/M2 ('a fold marks a PLACE') unanswered. TWO SPELLINGS FOR ONE ACT: these three plus ReferenceCard.tsx:25 write block ?? new $Block() and $check(typeOfX, '!') with a lowercase alias whose reason (the JSX shadow of 9f08552) died at 65b7053, while 37 bonds write $check(block, $Block) and pass the type class — ch15:17 records the alias spelling, so this is Doug's ruling, not a tidy. NO THEME COUPLING: no file here imports encyclopedia/; the only cross-folder coupling is book/ → reference/, ruled at 04-the-member-audit.md:58. Cosmetic residue: Bookmark.tsx:47 blank line (c8a29a2), PageFold.tsx:5-6 double blank (012283d). Run judgement: the kinds' substance is doug-driven (a37de5e, the margin's ten rounds verbatim; 8f29097 persist at his naming; 9f08552 and 012283d with Doug in the room); the port (65b7053) and the walk-away run (b384721) are long-self-guided and account for every silent drop.

## Cluster — src/reference (87 members)

**Cross-cutting:** ONE MISSING ASK, PAID SEVEN TIMES: 'the writing this holds that is not an annotation' is hand-scanned over _block.$elements at Reference.tsx:61-62, Catalogue.tsx:27-28 (comprehend), Catalogue.tsx:87-88 (held), ReferenceCard.tsx:16-17, and by class three more times in ReferenceCardSpecification (42-43, 52-53, 60). The find/findOne ruling (10-the-type-and-the-instance.md:204-220) counted this sentence twenty times, built searchFor/searchForOne asking BY TYPE, and there is no type for 'content' — so the ruling's own instrument cannot reach these seats; the gap is one level down. THE SIX-HEADINGS DISEASE IN THIS FOLDER (21-polymorphic-limiting.md:47): path() is byte-identical at Reference.tsx:25 and Ref.tsx:23; title() and get meaning are byte-identical at IndexCard.tsx:14/20 and book/CatalogueCard.tsx:12/18 because $CatalogueCard extends $Composition rather than $IndexCard and an implements clause cannot be satisfied by a type. THREE ANCHOR SITES for one semantic element, each fetching encyclopedia/AnchorFormat from a base folder: Reference.tsx:11, Ref.tsx:11, writing/Writing.tsx:11 — the same shape as the format imports ruled out today, unnamed in the list. RULES THAT DEMAND THE CLASS: RefSpecification asks `writing instanceof $Ref` twice (Ref.tsx:76, 81; introduced by the Sprint 39 walk-away run replacing $$(writing)($Ref)); ReferenceCardSpecification scans by class three times — all against Shells Over Types and 012283d's own 'written structurally, not with instanceof'. THE CATALOGUE'S ADDRESSING HALF IS DEAD CODE TODAY (read, not run): a mention is assigned only to paragraphs, sentences and words (Paragraph.tsx:47, Section.tsx:26, Sentence.tsx:39, Word.tsx:35; none in .wiki), so $Composition.catalogue() answers undefined for every book, chapter and section; $Ref.read() (Ref.tsx:43-45) therefore always throws, follow()'s descent (Catalogue.tsx:54) throws past one step, and address()/comprehend()/span() have zero callers; `.tests` holds no promise naming follow, address, comprehend, catalogue(), <Ref>, $IndexCard or $ReferenceCard. R13'S FOUR OVERRIDES ARE ALL IN THIS FOLDER (Reference.tsx:90, Path.tsx:34, Catalogue.tsx:104, IndexCard.tsx:34). DEAD TEXT: Catalogue.tsx:7-8 import $Reference/reference/$Path/path and use none (orphaned by ad7ec35; tsconfig has no noUnusedLocals); Reference.tsx:74-79 is a comment block under the no-comments ruling; Reference.tsx:50 repeats a $pid ??= the bond already settled. PROVENANCE PATTERN: the first-introducing commit for most current member TEXT is 65b7053 (Sprint 45, the v2.2 port — long-self-guided) or b384721 (Sprint 39 walk-away), while the designs underneath — reference as annotation, the card with a canonical first, comprehend, the address authority on the catalogue, the mention, meaning-of-the-title — are Doug's verbatim (35-the-margin:12-18, 33-the-handle:156, 34-the-index:82, 50-sprint-46:540/626/738, ad7ec35). The elegance is his; the instanceof rules, the protected hook nobody overrides (reads), the prop nobody passes ($path), the unreachable route (Ref.read) and the hand scans are what the runs did to fit his designs onto a base with no ask for held content and no way for a type to satisfy an implements clause.

## Cluster — src/writing (89 members)

**Cross-cutting:** SIX FILES, 443 LINES, ONE CLASS WRITTEN FIVE TIMES. Letter/Word/Sentence/Paragraph/Section each carry the same eight declarations — $X$, $X, $$X$, $$X, $TypeOfX, $TypeOf$X, XSpecification, $XSpecification — differing only in one regex, one below(), and whether makes() assigns a mention; 10-writing.md:363 called this 'one class written four times' of v1's $$X shadow, and the v2.2 port (65b7053, long-self-guided) reproduced the shape at eight declarations per level. That is the template Doug ruled for the LIBRARY (20-using-the-public-library:15), so it is not cruft — but every finding below is the same gap paid N times inside it. (1) THE SAME DEAD IMPORT IN FOUR FILES: `import { $TypeOfReference, ReferenceSpecification } from '@/reference/Reference'` at Letter.tsx:7, Sentence.tsx:8, Paragraph.tsx:10, Section.tsx:10 — grep shows each name occurs once per file (the import line). ad7ec35 (Sprint 48) retyped every `$TypeOf$X extends $TypeOfReference` to `extends $Type` and cleaned Word.tsx only. Cruft ×4, one edit. (2) THE MENTION IS ASSIGNED IN FOUR PLACES AND ITS LEVEL IS FIXED IN EVERY BOND, AGAINST D3. Word.tsx:35, Sentence.tsx:39, Paragraph.tsx:47 (in makes) and Section.tsx:24-26 (in the bond, `??=`, for authored paragraphs that never pass through makes). 50-sprint-46:191 D3 ruled the level DERIVED from the container via below() 'over fixing it in the bond, as $$Paragraph does today' — RISK K1, fallback stated — and the fallback is what ships. Three of the seven mention classes are NEVER MADE: $$Letter (Letter.tsx:30), $$Section (Section.tsx:32), and $$Chapter (Chapter.tsx:25) — grep '$($$Section)'/'$($$Letter)' across src/.wiki/.tests finds only the export lines; Book.tsx:97-104 builds its contents by reaching three levels into chapters instead of through $$Section (56-sprint-52:125 P2 names the reach). $$Letter additionally copies `sort`/`case` (Letter.tsx:31-32) to satisfy $$Letter$ extends $Letter$ — the same 'implements clause cannot be satisfied by a type' gap as heading(). (3) heading() IS ONE GAP PAID SIX TIMES — Section.tsx:18 is byte-identical in Author.tsx:12, Subject.tsx:12, Title.tsx:14, Table.tsx, Summary; ruled a design problem in 21-polymorphic-limiting:47-49 (2026-09-08). Book.tsx:103 does not even call it; it repeats the searchForOne. (4) THE LEAF RULES HOLD KNOWLEDGE THE PARSER ALSO HOLDS, AND ONE PAIR DISAGREES. SentenceSpecification.patterns.stopped `/[.!?][^\S\n]*\S/u` (Sentence.tsx:54) vs Parser.sentences' split `/(?<=[.!?])[^\S\n]+(?=\S)/u` (Parser.tsx:62): verified with node — 'Pi is 3.14 here.' and 'Visit example.com now.' are REFUSED by the rule and kept as ONE sentence by the parser. LetterSpecification.graphemes (Letter.tsx:81) is a second Intl.Segmenter beside Parser.graphemes (Parser.tsx:9). WordSpecification.patterns.broken (Word.tsx:50) beside parser.words' `/\s+/u` (Parser.tsx:72). Every leaf rule reads `html.text(writing._block)` and re-decides what the parser decides; the honest form is a rule that asks the parser (parser.sentences(parser.tokens(writing)).length === 1). And NO .tests promise names or quotes any of the five leaf rules ($oneCharacter, $noWhitespace, $stopsAtItsEnd, $noBlankLine, $onOneLine) — grep by name and by message text is empty; only the section's heading derivation is promised (.tests/writing.test.tsx:272-300). $noBlankLine and $onOneLine are also nearly unreachable from markup: 20-using-the-public-library:63 'JSX condenses the line breaks inside plain text to one space before anything of ours runs'. (5) $opensWithHeading (Section.tsx:65) WAS LOOSENED INTO VACUITY, UNRECORDED: 2b522b0 added `|| parser.tokens(writing).length > 0`, true for any section holding a token, so the rule refuses only what $saysSomething already refuses and cannot go red for the reason its name states — no sprint chapter records the change (grep 'opensWithHeading' in 55/56: a caller list only). The mechanism missing is a rule's way to read what supplies() answered without running the parse; the loosening hid that gap instead of naming it (21-polymorphic-limiting:55 'the defence is the finding'). (6) BASE-FOLDER → THEME-FOLDER COUPLING: Paragraph.tsx:4 `import { ProseFormat as prose } from '@/encyclopedia/ProseFormat'` and the frame() override at Paragraph.tsx:22 that wears it — delete-per-ruling under 57-sprint-53:44 T5 (the kind writes <p>) and :58 R3 ('grep -rn \"encyclopedia/\" src/writing src/book is empty' is the acceptance test). It is the only encyclopedia import in these six files; the `ReactNode` import at Paragraph.tsx:1 falls with it. (7) COMMENTS: Section.tsx:60-63, :71-74, :84-86 are the only comments in the six files (eleven lines, 2b522b0 and ad7ec35) — against the closed ruling at 11-the-coding-style:187 ('src proper stays bare') and the memory 'no code comments in lib'. The `measure = 60` proxy they flag (Section.tsx:87) stays owed a ruling and should be flagged in the library, not in a comment. (8) NAMES NEVER RULED: `sort` (Letter.tsx:20) replaced Doug's `kind` at ad7ec35 to dodge $Writing.kind (Writing.tsx:44) — unrecorded, and nothing reads sort/case anywhere (grep '\.sort\b'/'\.case\b': the writes at Letter.tsx:62-63 only); `spell`/`reads` (Letter.tsx:60,66) unflagged; `written` (Section.tsx:96) as the $-fetch local where the other five use the lowercase kind, forced by `export const section = $($$Section)` (Section.tsx:97), which has zero importers — D1 (50-sprint-46:189, Doug's) rules lowercase mention exports, 47-sprint-45:287 records his earlier 'assign one locally', and the six files split 2 (Section, Chapter) vs 4 (local Representation); `lines` (Phrase.tsx:22) where the other four say `patterns`, because a subclass cannot extend the inherited `patterns` table without breaking the inherited rule. Also $SectionSpecification stands ABOVE SectionSpecification (Section.tsx:56 vs :59), inverting the placement Doug gave (35-the-margin:93 '$XSpecification under the level's specification') that the other five obey. (9) THE BASES BENT, ALL SANCTIONED: Specification.supplies + $Annotation.supplies + parser.parse's `supply` (for Section.tsx:75, 52-sprint-48:165 U2 within R5's exception); $Writing.reading() + reflection.wrapped with its memo (for Section.tsx:20, Doug's wrap ruling 52:190/200, the memo after 55:209's 590-second hang); @inert() on $Writing.mention (for the four mention writes). Nothing in these six files touches $Writing.theme or `$check(theme, '!')` — today's theme ruling deletes nothing here. Chapter.tsx does not yet override reading() though the ruling names 'the section or chapter'. (10) RUN CHARACTER: the shape and the rules are Doug-driven (ebdadc7/e17be70 with Doug at the keyboard — 28-the-block:9; Sprint 41 ruled live — 43:5; Sprints 30-33 in the room — 33:5; the nine `$$X` his order — 35:89; Sprint 48's supplies/wrap his mid-sprint rulings — 52:16,61,190). The v2.2 port (65b7053, Sprint 45) that wrote these six files as they stand was long-self-guided, and the Sprint 46 mention implementation ran overnight (50:259 'THIRD INSTANCE OF THE SAME FAULT TONIGHT'; nine warts). Every cruft row above dates to one of those two runs or to the unrecorded 2b522b0 edit; every keep row dates to a ruling.

## Cluster — src/book (99 members)

**Cross-cutting:** Seven base-folder imports of the theme folder across these five files, and every one is struck by today's ruling: Book.tsx:22-26 (Body, Header, Sidebar, Content, Footer) and Chapter.tsx:12-13 (Article, Output); Part, Cover and Title import none. The Chapter one is the only member in these files that BENT THE BASE — $Type.format(drawn) (Type.tsx:14) and the reflection.formatted call in $Writing.frame (Writing.tsx:76) both entered at ad7ec35 so a type could wear encyclopedia formats. TWO SPRINT-46 RULINGS RECORDED AS DONE ARE NOT IN THE CODE: R12 (50:165, '$Book.contents() leaves $Book — Observed: Book.tsx no longer imports Heading, Paragraph, Ref or Section') against Book.tsx:10-13 and :95-115; R14 (50:169, 'placed() moves from protected on $Book to $Composition') against Book.tsx:117 — the demo night re-added both (49:187, 49:200), and Sprint 48 U4 ('placed() and following() go', 52:100) then stalled at 52:178-186 because the positional rules read the block and a cover cannot be supplied. THE SAME GAP PAID TWICE BY SUBTRACTION: searchFor($TypeOfChapter) answers every chapter KIND (Cover.tsx:26, Footer.tsx:15 extend $TypeOfChapter), so $Book.chapters (Book.tsx:51-52) subtracts five instances and $Part.chapters (Part.tsx:31-32) subtracts its own kind — the framework has no 'normal chapter'; 20-using-the-public-library makes the CONSUMER subclass one. THE BOND-ORDER SYMPTOM READ FROM THE CODE (not driven): `chapters` is captured at Book.tsx:60 and read by contents() at :101 before footer is assigned at :65, so an authored footer is counted as a chapter — in the table of contents, in _body (:64) and in _closing (:66); the working copy authors one (.wiki/alan-turing/.margin.tsx). THE SAME STRAY TWICE: `$TypeOfReference, ReferenceSpecification` imported and unread at Book.tsx:16 and Chapter.tsx:11 (orphaned when ad7ec35 made the mention types plain), plus an unread `reflection` at Chapter.tsx:4 — 49:203 claims these were swept. $Part HAS NO CONSUMER: only src/index.ts:35 exports it; nothing in src, .wiki or .tests constructs one; src/article/ that Sprint 51 U4 names is empty; the file was born in a stopped, chapterless session (49:183) and its three fields are filled in specifically() (Part.tsx:29-34), the verifier, with the parameter narrowed to the class — a type assuming an instance (21 rule 3) and the assignment in the checker (11 § the-assignment-workflow). heading() at Title.tsx:14 is byte-identical to Section.tsx:18 — ruling 21's sixth copy, forced by $Title$ extends $Section$ while $Title extends $Composition. The five book fields typed $Writing (shells over types, by design) cost the consumer a cast: `book.cover as $Cover` at .tests/authoring.test.tsx:44. Runs: the four blocks, the region view, the region imports, placed(), $tableStandsThird, Part.tsx and the Title heading-from-copy all reach the repo through 012283d (2026-09-07), a commit carrying the 2026-09-06 demo night ('went off trail by design and never earned a chapter while it ran', 49:8; 'coined tonight', 49:303) and a stopped session before it (49:183); footer, following(), contents(after), $endsWithFooter and $TypeOfChapter.format come through ad7ec35 the same day ('THIRD INSTANCE OF THE SAME FAULT TONIGHT', 50:259). Everything Doug-driven in these files is either the template (interface · class · type · specification, bonds, exports), a rule he spoke (cover/synopsis/table/footer presence, the cover's three, a title that means and makes its heading), or Sprint 51's U7 — and everything struck is what those two night commits added around them.

## Cluster — library/.public/package/src/utilities (111 members)

**Cross-cutting:** ONE RING, TWO MECHANISMS. Reflection cannot value-import $Writing/$Annotation/$Type because Writing.tsx:4 and Type.tsx:5 import reflection, so it is handed them through kinds!/knows() (Reflection.tsx:17-25), $Type.$register (Type.tsx:28), a generated block in src/index.ts:56-60, register.ts, and a prebuild script — five pieces, one registrant. Parser.tsx:3-4 imports the same two classes as values with no ring at all, because nothing it serves imports it back. The ring exists only because $Writing delegates questions about itself to reflection (Writing.tsx:35-37 meaning/annotations/type); move those home and the whole apparatus loses its reason. Chemistry already holds a registry keyed on the component object (41-sprint-39:81). This is the ceremony 56-sprint-52#ceremony names, in the build. THE UTILITIES ARE PREDICATES IN EXILE, AND THEY PAY FOR IT LINE BY LINE. 29-the-bind.md R339 said it of html.text; it is now true of the folder: _block is read from outside the class on Parser.tsx:12,80, Reflection.tsx:55,62,66,70,117 and Html.ts:10; a $Type is matched by the STRING 'Reference' (Reflection.tsx:71) and the answer cast back (Writing.tsx:35); a roster of the seven level names (Reflection.tsx:27) answers wrong for 26 of 40 types (56-sprint-52:156); wrapped() takes `$Writing & { parts() }` (Reflection.tsx:49) because the utility's own type import lacks parts; indent() walks `at: any` (Reflection.tsx:102); Html.text duck-types the library's own classes by member name (Html.ts:9-10). Each is the same gap paid again: a utility that must know the classes it may not name. THREE CACHES, ONE PERMITTED. Parser.parts caches parts — the one thing Doug's 2026-09-08 ruling allows. Reflection.templates caches hand-`new`'d $Type instances with `_block ??= new $Block()` poked in (Reflection.tsx:115-120) because name/below/makes are instance members of $Type (Type.tsx:10-13) — structure that 21-polymorphic-limiting says belongs ON the class. Reflection.readings caches a $Block per section built on the draw path (wrapped() calls parts() at :50 and constructs at :53, violating 14-shells-over-types.md#the-view-law), added to stop a 590s hang (55-sprint-51:209) — directly against 'We do NOT cache lots of blocks'. DEAD WEIGHT, COUNTED. Url.ts: 7 of 9 methods and 2 of 4 patterns have no caller in src, .tests or .wiki — reads (Path.tsx:31) and addresses (Reference.tsx:39) are the whole live surface. Specification.parent is never assigned. Reflection.indent reads $Writing.$indent (Writing.tsx:14,29), a $-prefixed reactive field on the base that NOTHING writes, so classNames never emits pd-indent-N and the encyclopedia's `.pd-indent-1/-2` selectors (ContentFormat.tsx:14, SidebarFormat.tsx:20,22) never match; 43-sprint-41:400 ruled indent 'asked rather than stored' and the code stores. knownCycles[0] names a Composition↔Catalogue cycle the source no longer has (Composition.tsx:6 is type-only; the introducing commit says so) under a comment claiming both are runtime cycles. katex, @types/katex (rollup :51, package.json :48-49) and react-router-dom (:55, rollup :51) survive from code deleted in 65b7053. The './utilities' door (package.json:26, rollup :67,:89-94, src/utilities/index.ts) has zero consumers; the './encyclopedia' door duplicates '.' because src/index.ts:49 re-exports the theme through the base entry. react-dom is a `dependency` no src file imports. package.json:4's em dash is double-encoded (C3 A2 E2 82 AC E2 80 9D). tsx and vite are run by scripts :36,:42,:43 and declared only in sibling workspaces. THE RUN THAT WROTE MOST OF THIS MIS-COUNTED ITSELF. Commit ad7ec35 (2026-09-07, 'Sprint 48: the mention is the mention…') says 'NINE MEMBERS DELETED, NONE ADDED' and introduced UrlParser (9 methods), Reflection.kinds/knows/wrapped/formatted, Specification.supplies, Parser's supply parameter, register.ts, the registrations block, knownCycles and the three-door build. Nothing in projection/ or designing-inexplicable-phenomena/ records a ruling for register.ts, knows(), UrlParser or knownCycles (greps empty). Under the criterion's own instruction — be skeptical of long ones — every design-problem row above that cites ad7ec35 is a member Doug did not rule. BASE-TO-THEME COUPLING AT THE DOORS AND IN THE SHEET. src/index.ts:49 ships the encyclopedia through the lib entry; Writing.tsx:11 imports $AnchorFormat into the base (another owner's file, registered here as the coupling this folder's export row depends on). Of the 16 encyclopedia exports, five are structural under today's ruling (Body grid + display: contents, Margin gridArea, Table grid + contents, Illustration figure/figcaption, Footer by inheritance); four are named today as base imports that go (Bullets, Columns, Heading, Prose, plus Output/Article via $TypeOfChapter.format); the rest are sheet declarations on pd- classes that the GitHub sheet takes as nested @select groups. A STRAY INSTALL INSIDE SRC. library/.public/package/src/encyclopedia/node_modules (esbuild, vite; untracked, gitignored, 14 .ts files) is walked by register.ts:15-24 on every prebuild, and its `/\.tsx?$/` also matches `.d.ts` — the prebuild reads vite's type definitions looking for `static $register`. It also produced the only name-grep hit for `url.path`, which is how one dead member briefly looked alive.

## Cluster — src/writing (89 members)

**Cross-cutting:** THE THEME CHURN IS THE CLUSTER'S STORY. ch13 (2026-09-02) said the palette is an ANNOTATION found in the document. Then four shapes in four days, each the implementer's: a lazy walk (d348a28, night), a cached `_theme` (68d8365, night), a field plus `$check(theme, '!')` in every bond (ad7ec35, after Doug's 'just make it a simple property'), and today T2 restores ch13's read. Every shape but the first ruling was a long run reading a symptom (50-sprint-46 § verdict P7: 'THE DESIGN WAS RIGHT AND MY SHORTCUT WAS WRONG'). DUPLICATED MEMBERS: `theme!: $Theme` and `this.theme = $check(theme, '!')` stand byte-identical in $Writing (Writing.tsx:33,54) and $Format (Format.tsx:9,12) — one gap paid twice; $Composition.view() and .frame() (Composition.tsx:48-54) carry the same `parenthetical && !$print` conditional twice — four members (two flags, two overrides) for the one holder-supplied mechanism Sprint 51 names and Doug has not ruled. ONE IMPORT CYCLE PAID THREE WAYS: writing/ must ask `instanceof` against itself through reflection, reflection cannot import writing, so `$Type.$register` (a static on a domain class, against 1a5515f's statics law), `reflection.knows` (a late-filled slot), and `package/register.ts` (an emitter that rewrites index.ts) all exist — plus `import type` edges to reference/ (Writing.tsx:7, Composition.tsx:6) of the exact kind ch11 § supervision lists as done without asking on 2026-09-05. BASE→THEME COUPLING in these six files is one line, Writing.tsx:11 (AnchorFormat), and R3's grep deletes it. A SEAM WITH ONE USER: `$Type.format()` + `reflection.formatted` in `$Writing.frame` serve only `$TypeOfChapter.format`, which T4/R3 delete; then the seam formats nothing and T6 puts formats in the block as annotations. LEVEL-BY-STRING: `$Type.name` + reflection's roster ['Book','Chapter',…] + `below()` chains carry level-hood the class lattice cannot express (56-sprint-52 measured 26 of 40 types reported as levels; Doug's own question whether LEVEL belongs on $Composition). ZERO-CALLER MEMBERS: addType, where, selectMany, single, $view('back'), the `composition` alias, the `$Composition` bond that only calls super. DEAD FIELD: `$Theme.atom = true` — chemistry reads no `atom` member; its singleton is the `$Atom` class, which cannot be combined with T1's `extends $Annotation` — a ruling owed. STALE COMMENTS crossing the no-comments rule: Writing.tsx:140-144 (describes a parts() read 2b522b0 removed) and Type.tsx:24-27. RUN SKEW, measured on this register: every cruft and design-problem verdict traces to 65b7053 (the port), 012283d/d348a28/68d8365 (the mention night, 01:57–03:08), b384721 (the walk-away run), the unruled additions inside ad7ec35, or 2b522b0's `atom`; every keep traces to a Doug ruling with a file:line — which is the distribution Doug predicted ('Assume the elegance comes from me').

## Cluster — src/encyclopedia (211 members)

**Cross-cutting:** SHEET-ABLE vs STRUCTURAL, per format (T3/T5/T6, 57-sprint-53:42-45): entirely sheet-able — AnchorFormat (a, a:hover; the `<a>` becomes the kind's under T5), ArticleFormat (article), BulletsFormat (ul/li/.pd-list), CitedFormat (ol; zero consumers anywhere), ColumnsFormat (.pd-index column-count), HeadingFormat (h2; $id goes with T5), OutputFormat (one `> *:first-child` rule), ProseFormat (p), HeaderFormat (seventeen rules all on .pd-title/.pd-author/.pd-subject/.pd-synopsis — already the sheet's shape). Structural — BodyFormat (named-area grid, `main`), MarginFormat/$at (grid areas), TableFormat (grid + display:contents + the per-instance $columns), IllustrationFormat (figure; T6 names it). Split — ContentFormat (gridArea main structural; heading_/sub_ sheet-able), SidebarFormat (sticky region structural; heading_/entry_/link_/hover_ sheet-able), FooterFormat (region structural; .pd-index sheet-able). pd- CLASSES TARGETED: Body → .pd-book; Bullets → .pd-list; Content → .pd-indent-1; Footer → .pd-index; Header → .pd-title, .pd-author, .pd-subject, .pd-synopsis; Sidebar → .pd-indent-1, .pd-indent-2; Table → .pd-table, .pd-heading; the other nine target none (elements only). All pd- classes are minted at Reflection.tsx:107-111 onto the `<span>` $Writing.frame wraps (Writing.tsx:75-78). IMPORTS FROM writing/ AND book/ (grep 2026-09-08): Paragraph:4 Prose, Heading:8 Heading, List:8 Bullets, Table:9 Table, Illustration:8 Illustration, Writing:11 Anchor; Book:22-26 Body/Header/Sidebar/Content/Footer, Chapter:12-13 Article/Output, Index:7 Columns; plus reference/Ref:11 and Reference:11 Anchor (outside R3's grep but inside T9's default door). Four formats — Cited, Header, Margin, Footer, Sidebar, Content — are imported by NO writing/ file; nine are imported by exactly one base file each. THE SAME GAP PAID N TIMES: (1) the `<span>` frame — five `display:block` rules (Footer index_display, Header byline_display, Sidebar sub_/deep_display) and two `display:contents` rules (Body book_display, Table table_display) exist only to make or pierce that span; T5 removes the cause of all seven. (2) One `h2` is styled in FOUR files: HeadingFormat sets serif/rule/margin/padding, and ContentFormat (heading_, sub_), HeaderFormat (title_, bylineHeading_) and SidebarFormat (heading_) each undo two to four of them — the default holds only for the chapter heading; a single sheet says `h2` and `.pd-indent-N h2` and `.pd-title h2` once. (3) `color: ink` is declared at the Body root and repeated in seven getters (Article, Bullets, Cited, Prose, Table, Illustration caption, Header author) — inherited anyway. (4) The anchor's rules are restated in Sidebar (link_textDecoration, hover_color) and undone in Header (cover_ group), beside two orphan props (showMeaning, clickableMeaning) added for the same case that 50-sprint-46:1095 solved by deleting views. (5) Two breakpoint literals (1119px in Body and Sidebar; 480px in Illustration) live in three files with no theme token. THE ONE MECHANISM THAT IS THE REGISTER'S FINDING: $MarginFormat + $at + gridArea + BodyFormat's areas + $Book's four block slices and hand view are one design (ch21:45 'the pattern entire'); T8 strikes the book half, which leaves the format half — six files — as encyclopedia apparatus with no consumer in src (56-sprint-52:77), to be re-hung on the encyclopedia book kind or on Parts. A CHEMISTRY FACT TO VERIFY IN THE DOM BEFORE THE NEXT DRIVE (read at styled.ts:341-347, not driven): given() forwards every non-framework `$`-prop with the `$` stripped, so `$at` and `$columns` — props that only feed getters — reach the div as `at=\"left\"` / `columns=\"2\"` attributes; the blend was designed for href (styled.ts:335). A second chemistry fact shaped MarginFormat's `div`: a styled subclass naming its own element re-roots and loses the base's getters (49:250), which is why header/aside/footer were given up — a pitch item under T5. PROVENANCE IN ONE LINE: the Wikipedia numbers and the element mapping are Doug's (35-the-margin.md:124-127, Sprint 40 'ruling live throughout'); the FORMAT-CLASS split-out and every `$XFormat` export is the v2.2 port's (65b7053, long self-guided, and it split $Anchor out of Writing.tsx against ch13:47's ruling); the five region formats and their ~60 CSS members are the Wikipedia demo session's (49-the-wikipedia-demo.md, a brainstorm 'off trail by design' that Doug stopped, then minimized on his word), with only their skeleton ($at, the grid, the table grid) on his checklist; IllustrationFormat (ad7ec35) has no record at all; the @select prefix names are proxies (11-styled-particles.md:139). Working tree matches HEAD 2b522b0 for all sixteen files.
